1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The optics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 599 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 193 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 599 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the optics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 193. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
